##### What was that? TikTok fights AI slop + doomscrolling, Youtube brings back DMs, Meta wins antitrust case, Search & ChatGPT deliver no clicks, and the rest is chaos for marketers and consumers.

This newsletter is supported by our friends at [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/97584f36-558c-4991-9929-ee1df63ebe81?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the affordable, always-on brand tracking dashboard.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!VaNH!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/4f542216-2555-41e7-b662-6668a323e59e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [TikTok](https://substack.com/redirect/8aed2f32-7e57-42b4-ba95-5d7b0124317e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to give users power to [reduce amount of AI content](https://substack.com/redirect/45063b12-ea4c-413b-bc62-a7158af553c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on their feeds.
* YouTube is [bringing back DMs](https://substack.com/redirect/2c769a5f-00c2-42f1-b362-49a25eb69d10?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after 6 years, but there’s a catch.
* [X’s staying power in the U.S.](https://substack.com/redirect/63fbc66f-3a98-4e7a-b7a3-d9e1463e55c1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), despite competition.
	+ According to [Pew’s latest social media report](https://substack.com/redirect/1bf34328-6315-4c4b-8f2d-108b293d4027?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), 21% of U.S. adults said they have used X, compared with only 8% who have used Threads, and 4% who have used Bluesky.
* Adobe to buy digital marketing platform [Semrush](https://substack.com/redirect/61b62570-d00f-42bf-b89d-cf61faa7c3ac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in $1.9 billion deal.
* Meta [defeats](https://substack.com/redirect/49829708-4131-4ed1-9b9f-bd82482dc669?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) FTC’s antitrust case [alleging](https://substack.com/redirect/b1dda70a-03c0-41ca-a167-6f2f90478d7c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) social-media monopoly.
* [Gemini 3](https://substack.com/redirect/ca53d33c-5505-4770-9fcf-cbf547a1bee6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is here—and Google says it will make [search](https://substack.com/redirect/43d05af6-04e6-4460-b2c8-1265a3358d3c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) smarter.

* Owner of [U.K.’s Daily Mail](https://substack.com/redirect/1b7b6459-6649-494d-b02c-faf3e522a5c3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) nears deal to buy rival Daily Telegraph.

	+ Meet the [neo-Nazi targeting kids online](https://substack.com/redirect/521ca11f-bc80-49bb-8e60-d1affb777b7e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), teaching them to hate and to prepare to kill.
* Thousands of french brands sue [Shein](https://substack.com/redirect/66bf371c-795c-451b-b2f9-9c15119195ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), accusing it of unfair competition.

> *In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/8493a1be-e720-4fab-93bf-f0a0986954c9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* 

##### **Gen Z darling, Depop is betting on a broader audience**

Secondhand clothing resale platform, Depop recently launched their biggest ever brand campaign, “[Where Taste Recognizes Taste](https://substack.com/redirect/5d4968ab-58ed-45c4-8edc-a2f2b86407ee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)”, made with Uncommon Creative Studio. It’s the brand’s attempt to expand its customer base, trying to shift its positioning slightly away from purely “for Gen Zs” who have become the unofficial ambassadors of the platform.

[Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/d1f92aa0-163e-49a9-8e49-94ef49ea7063?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is tracking **Depop** in a few categories, and in the Women’s Secondhand Apparel (US) category, all of Depop’s funnel metrics have increased from November 2024 to October 2025. However, they are well and truly a challenger, with the lowest awareness in the category at 21% – compared to the likes of ebay at 80% and Poshmark at 70%.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!y25Q!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F25f12daa-7edf-4b7c-ad8b-d34037d3d151_1738x972.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/164c15fd-3e75-4d32-8426-6b143ea993b8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)Despite being lower on the overall awareness front, Depop have obviously found their people; these brand health metrics dramatically shift when you segment by the Gen Z demographic. Amongst 18 to 24-year-olds, Awareness increases to 57%, Consideration to 47% and Preference to 29%. We can also see that [these metrics](https://substack.com/redirect/d1f92aa0-163e-49a9-8e49-94ef49ea7063?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) have increased a lot in the last 12 months in this age group.

In a marketplace category that can’t differentiate all too much on functionality and product, the value of brand is further elevated. Brand tracking tools like [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/d1f92aa0-163e-49a9-8e49-94ef49ea7063?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are here to help marketers prove that big brand campaigns have paid off, and with the right audiences. Because no one can call brand fluffy, when you’ve got the growth receipts!

Discovery.

It’ll come up at earnings.

* Delayed September report shows [U.S. added 119,000 jobs](https://substack.com/redirect/2931a044-f5fc-4206-b132-d832665b651e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), more than expected; unemployment rate at 4.4%.
* [Study](https://substack.com/redirect/6b488f18-1926-4df9-b09e-8561b3326ae6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): Anthropic AI Model ‘[turned evil](https://substack.com/redirect/ac4cb68b-b6ce-4682-95c4-30a91f1a6c33?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ after hacking its training.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!BaXH!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6a655c94-ab41-4b83-8ee6-37cf93a7d7bb_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/2c0874c5-682a-4262-9a10-4a7c990b25fe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Walmart’s [ad business](https://substack.com/redirect/7d4a99d9-ff17-4fc5-aa89-eeb4913ac7db?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) continues to accelerate, growing 53% in Q3.

* Europe begins [rethinking its crackdown](https://substack.com/redirect/c080cfea-7834-43ff-a01c-64d684b33d56?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on Big Tech.
	+ We tried to get Big Tech to pay for [wrecking journalism](https://substack.com/redirect/72084a84-91b3-4e96-a52c-50ceb7729869?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). It didn’t work out.
* What will it take to get [retailers to kill the music](https://substack.com/redirect/6a52df3e-afc4-44dd-ae96-8d6d31da43da?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)?
	+ Why [fan edits](https://substack.com/redirect/151f26cb-9374-49c4-a4b1-6959b2644364?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are movie marketing magic.
* Forget sports betting. [Kalshi](https://substack.com/redirect/7bcf5426-41db-4f98-9dbf-659ef59bdbca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) wants you to wager on sneakers and Labubu dolls.
* Investigating [a possible scammer](https://substack.com/redirect/bfd385cc-af29-4188-a244-5671d8ce52ae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in journalism’s AI era.
* Bro boost: women say their [LinkedIn traffic increases](https://substack.com/redirect/16004257-ea8e-4eb9-8f03-3ed637b8a914?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) if they pretend to be men.
	+ LinkedIn [denies](https://substack.com/redirect/deb140d8-8469-4944-a2ec-3706a043a80b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) gender bias in determining post reach.
* [The Brazilian Novelist](https://substack.com/redirect/aaf6364d-71da-4e97-b629-13174186845b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) getting Gen Z into books.
* Amazon [quietly blocks](https://substack.com/redirect/58f7b75d-d94c-41ee-86b8-a4b81c1b3bea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) more of OpenAI’s ChatGPT web crawlers from accessing its site.
* Study: Social media use drives [distrust](https://substack.com/redirect/e9431fa1-16d3-451d-b308-d0f7b292f949?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) among Gen Z teenage girls.
* [TIME Magazine](https://substack.com/redirect/8072df36-d52e-441c-a641-ea8197b0c8f6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) partners with [Galactic](https://substack.com/redirect/6fe396ef-2cd1-4e07-90f0-7e822040bfc2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to launch prediction market platform.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!dE8f!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fc0b0e67a-ad8c-4778-a9ee-8066b4dda5be_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/f4e9faa1-0170-4ae6-bbdc-bf0f0904c49d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* TikTok and Instagram Reels cause [real brainrot](https://substack.com/redirect/33909291-6f6d-4b87-87d1-7fac9c79720c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), major study finds.
* I got results! Meta [fixed](https://substack.com/redirect/49523d14-0bfc-41f4-a591-9ba92a83e9a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Instagram links in Slack.

	+ The [simple test](https://substack.com/redirect/722bd008-5885-46c2-9d6e-53d50a45525a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that blew up the FTC’s case against Meta.
* Meta enhances [HDR](https://substack.com/redirect/b2d7018e-a0f1-4199-9dcf-d25b1d390846?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on Instagram for iOS with Dolby Vision.
* Instagram is testing on iOS a new “[Expiring soon](https://substack.com/redirect/3ed39656-cf2f-46e9-90d1-a55eafc14f78?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” label for stories.
* Instagram is currently testing a feature that lets you [choose different font styles](https://substack.com/redirect/f59be6ca-7ce2-43c7-92ac-c1258dc9bbcf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Notes.
* Threads is testing a new “[checkmark](https://substack.com/redirect/47151518-6576-487c-8244-24783e7eda12?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” label feature for replies left by post’s author.

> ⚰️ Report Break: A leaked file reveals almost [no one is clicking](https://substack.com/redirect/4df78444-02e0-410d-9f83-7f095f7dfc0e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on ChatGPT links.

> 610,775 total link impressions / 4,238 total clicks / 0.69% overall CTR.

> Over two in five consumers (42%) say they [trust AI-generated summaries](https://substack.com/redirect/c4d61bb2-a5b3-4b0b-8f06-d8ed6686d59e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) without visiting a website, according to another research from Optimizely. 62% of marketers believe click-less journeys will become the norm within the next year or are already happening, yet only 27% feel well prepared for this new reality.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!VXYk!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F54241e92-dc00-45e3-b0a1-422988e5b58f_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/6a7b427e-2d51-4024-8f09-18334801d203?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Meta launches [new content protection tools](https://substack.com/redirect/a1aebe45-cb82-4089-8f0e-b845d116e645?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to help creators fight against content thieves and aggregators.
* [WhatsApp](https://substack.com/redirect/4264ce3a-9f08-4dc4-943a-9026e0d9b997?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is bringing back its very first feature: [About](https://substack.com/redirect/48fb0d62-465c-469c-aa18-8ecd7fd021bd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Spanish court orders Meta to [pay $550 million](https://substack.com/redirect/88dfa8d8-d9bd-477b-a834-5ed8e2115373?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to digital media companies.
* Meta buried ‘causal’ [evidence of social media harm](https://substack.com/redirect/9f0bf167-5ede-4671-b609-0cc91e1e41eb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), US court filings allege.
* With upcoming ban, Meta begins to [notify Australian teens](https://substack.com/redirect/f7b0d5d4-8237-43fd-b829-67eaaa2ef4a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that their accounts will be shut down.
* Meta tests [virtual try-on](https://substack.com/redirect/683eabd1-416a-49fb-bbeb-48f7079f0b70?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for furniture on marketplace.
* Internal documents show [how many people are using Meta’s Vibes AI feed](https://substack.com/redirect/d9437243-a361-48ee-85cd-90d4c54b19ee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) each day.
* Messenger users will now have the ability to automatically verify the authenticity of their [contacts’ encryption keys](https://substack.com/redirect/83cfdc4e-a528-469f-9cf1-7d44d390efec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for one-on-one chats.
* WhatsApp may show Facebook and Instagram usernames for [unknown numbers](https://substack.com/redirect/76f4621b-15a0-4b04-8694-65115adba2f3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Meta chief AI scientist Yann LeCun is [leaving](https://substack.com/redirect/ef9f66cd-a794-4a03-b4f3-df1025fb5179?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the company.
	+ Meta [Chief Revenue Officer](https://substack.com/redirect/f1fd2c1a-c09e-4328-8645-298f915509b1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is also leaving to form own startup.
* Meta advances towards the [metaverse](https://substack.com/redirect/047da93a-97a1-4f77-a226-18055391545f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with new creative tools.
* UK Department for Education faces backlash over ‘[apology statement](https://substack.com/redirect/2dc6ad02-8e25-408b-89d6-7699d059eec4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ on Facebook.
* Zuckerberg, Meta directors agree to [$190 million settlement](https://substack.com/redirect/95817d3f-302f-4f10-8027-cb75b1e67e48?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of shareholder privacy case.
* [Oversight Board](https://substack.com/redirect/637e3a9b-f517-4848-936f-8a982eef1d51?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to review Meta’s plans to expand Community Notes [globally](https://substack.com/redirect/26c407a7-f8a0-48e6-9477-a43aa2e0d1e9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Tit-For-Tat: Porn Producers counter Meta’s “[Personal Use](https://substack.com/redirect/4b2a979b-abce-490e-8f35-e101349beb12?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” piracy defense.
* Meta completes core of [2Africa](https://substack.com/redirect/4647f02c-17d2-4068-a3da-b10702418cbd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) subsea cable.
* Meta’s [new image segmentation models](https://substack.com/redirect/c27a09bd-f101-4cb5-9335-3f7ae9669980?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) can identify objects and people and reconstruct them in 3D.
* I visited [Meta’s NYC pop-up](https://substack.com/redirect/c5b6ff7b-a0a3-4672-adbd-1d88a6b33727?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). It felt like a mix of an Apple store and Warby Parker, with no expense spared.

> 👀 Report Break: Backlash grows against AI slop, but marketers remain [unfazed](https://substack.com/redirect/5bab9d6f-5d5c-4b64-94ce-97bdec0d0d58?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

> According to a recent BeReal survey, the social media platform reports that 47% of Gen Zers donʼt like [AI-generated content](https://substack.com/redirect/5bab9d6f-5d5c-4b64-94ce-97bdec0d0d58?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), and prefer human-created content. Meanwhile, research from influencer shop Billion Dollar Boy reports that users “respond when AI enhances creativity, but not when it’s used to churn out repetitive, low-quality output.”

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!xdrk!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F504f7135-86f7-4edd-a923-b08f71d3eeb3_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/594216b7-0a7c-439d-9d26-bbdbf1652e1b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* YouTube’s [right-wing stars](https://substack.com/redirect/c864d025-8f8c-4a9c-85ad-d2488b0d6f89?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) fuel boom in politically charged ads.
	+ The Brands [propping up](https://substack.com/redirect/8efff299-c20c-479b-8fd4-c66a04039104?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) right-wing YouTube.
* YouTube TV’s ‘[lower-cost sports bundle](https://substack.com/redirect/880db3c0-1d83-4c79-8a1d-0039b807a6fb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ possibly coming next year, MLB.TV excluded.
* ‘[Baby Shark Dance](https://substack.com/redirect/6e18b469-da38-48e7-aaac-df8b86555799?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g),’ world’s most-watched YouTube video hasn’t made its creator rich.
	+ I’m [officially done](https://substack.com/redirect/3a7a4b39-9a8d-40b3-906f-261aeaa2bef7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with YouTube Kids.
* TV distributors turned YouTube into a home for reruns. Now it’s hosting [unaired episodes](https://substack.com/redirect/415a4590-d2de-4353-a6c7-a7f69e18e894?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), too.
* [IMG Report](https://substack.com/redirect/4e57401b-e738-4c24-a024-0c12119e8b2d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): YouTube the priority platform [for](https://substack.com/redirect/1cd58c66-5ed2-4037-9895-8c3818eef8d5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) sports industry.
	+ More is more is more: The age of quality over quantity is over. Sports brands can no longer rely on infrequent, polished content.
* YouTube Music appears to be testing a new feature that allows users to [search within playlists](https://substack.com/redirect/751b1f51-fd99-46c9-b6ac-34b75d4595ca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* YouTube unveils [new content](https://substack.com/redirect/9925730f-530c-4744-adbe-b43a1181ae8a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) coming from Ms. Rachel, Trevor Noah, Dhar Mann and more.
* YouTube [improves](https://substack.com/redirect/d972774f-00aa-449e-9a38-2c434beb69df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) timestamp controls, expands reply suggestions.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!SRmL!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6ddeabac-2fb9-4449-8c6d-6f3b735dce87_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/6a5fec6f-d1ea-4227-823c-eed7c0b21c2b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [Google](https://substack.com/redirect/a4d2ebca-6aa5-4c5c-bcea-795c348b6db4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) expands [ads within AI Mode](https://substack.com/redirect/ae7ff5f2-101e-4a50-ac5c-1cf0d8a864be?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Google brings [Gemini 3](https://substack.com/redirect/78890565-1b57-4cfe-8c31-c0c14d60de62?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to Search’s AI Mode.
* Google [Antigravity](https://substack.com/redirect/b4e59ce7-14b5-43e3-b6a7-af1f03a17228?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is an ‘agent-first’ coding tool built for Gemini 3.
* Google is [fighting](https://substack.com/redirect/b82e57c0-7a6c-4384-b333-0c2959a3c850?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the defamation battle Meta caved on.
* Google tells employees it must [double capacity every 6 months](https://substack.com/redirect/802cd757-7c3f-4645-b1a8-12bd7228b5f0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to meet AI demand.
* Google Search Console adds [custom annotations](https://substack.com/redirect/7271bfca-01f6-4692-941a-de7bb892b400?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to reports.
* Google Search Console [adds](https://substack.com/redirect/5015ae52-6e01-4dfe-ad27-1537826c5122?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) branded queries filter.
* Google steps up [AI scam protection](https://substack.com/redirect/300f7a0a-39ee-403e-b7cd-c335f54d4430?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in India, but gaps remain.
* Google [CTR trends](https://substack.com/redirect/35f38d8e-af16-4e32-a688-18de020bdd60?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Q3: Branded clicks fan out, longer queries hold.
	+ For searches containing a brand or business name, position 1 lost 1.52 percentage points of CTR. Positions 2 through 6 gained a combined 8.71 points.
* Google [introduces](https://substack.com/redirect/4c2c37e5-0203-4bd0-b8dc-6b67eef0796f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) brand suitability controls, A/B experiments, video enhancements, and AI image tools for Demand Gen.

---

* Google cracked [Apple’s AirDrop](https://substack.com/redirect/32055425-398d-41f3-b627-92eb51841d22?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and is adding it to Pixel phones.
* Google Ads quietly rolls out a [new conversion metric](https://substack.com/redirect/25303153-f971-429d-8323-e23bcb9e63c1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Google launches [Nano Banana Pro](https://substack.com/redirect/33eed075-1281-403e-8b19-a3df4d2a2813?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), an updated AI image generator powered by Gemini 3.
* Google Maps adds [know before you go](https://substack.com/redirect/d081e3ba-9eac-4da7-aa19-588b3039407e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), trending in explore tab and more.
* Google works on [Live Preview](https://substack.com/redirect/610d7bdf-33f3-47a8-aabd-eb668fd2f8b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and Slack integration for Jules.
* Google AI Mode will help you create [travel plans](https://substack.com/redirect/7d8810d9-093e-4e8f-b9cd-b981000cf4a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), agentic booking launches.
* Google Ads adds [account-level political content](https://substack.com/redirect/880fabd1-e300-4ff7-9223-6a8f5bbe217f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) setting.
* Google offers [voluntary buyouts](https://substack.com/redirect/efd1cd61-97b4-4334-91ae-cdbabe47a1b4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to UK employees.
* Google tests “[Journey Aware Bidding](https://substack.com/redirect/d3c808a4-d0c0-4341-bd80-3ce1d1b68104?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” to optimize Search campaigns.

> [PEW](https://substack.com/redirect/1bf34328-6315-4c4b-8f2d-108b293d4027?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): Half of adults say they use Instagram, making it the only other platform in our survey used by at least 50% of Americans.

> Smaller shares use the other sites and apps we asked about, such as TikTok (37%) and WhatsApp (32%). Somewhat fewer say the same of Reddit, Snapchat and X (formerly Twitter).

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!J7DY!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Facd06a12-7508-4bfa-91b1-8c434a20fc84_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/3429fe70-b621-45c9-a2f9-31e1f4bbcbaf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* X [removed](https://substack.com/redirect/df778018-f582-4ed7-be37-8faf9104722e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the new account location feature on Saturday [after it revealed](https://substack.com/redirect/3d4532c4-3ca9-4bb8-9eba-c5338e3dc29b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that many extremist and Musk-fan accounts run [from](https://substack.com/redirect/9cb7699a-a12e-4672-898a-c3f5d8864d42?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) foreign engagement farms. 
	+ Update: “[About this Account](https://substack.com/redirect/06e99603-1eff-4e92-95f2-660625580104?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” is back and X plans to provide many more ways for users to verify the authenticity of the content they see on X. As many real users have [reported](https://substack.com/redirect/a82870d8-f418-4a6f-95b5-32df597cf82b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that X is showing the [wrong location](https://substack.com/redirect/a3c5081b-bd43-4f95-a544-6b34e753903a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for their accounts.
* xAI [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/4e1099c5-5fa4-484b-8a3e-a765425e2b46?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Grok 4.1 [with](https://substack.com/redirect/9e8f16d0-35bf-4164-ac09-75e47a74de15?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) lower hallucination rate.
	+ Musk’s xAI to build [500 Megawatt data center](https://substack.com/redirect/ff22d8b7-0d09-460c-88e2-9f141611df57?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Saudi Arabia.
* [Shocker](https://substack.com/redirect/6994c0ab-3062-465c-bbaf-d5a48eac4f95?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): Elon Musk spends a lot of time on X posting bad political takes.
* Can crowdchecking curb misinformation? [Evidence](https://substack.com/redirect/d08554fb-2791-4988-b32e-c7fa26a9366d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from Community Notes.
* Elon Musk’s Grok AI tells users he is [fitter than LeBron James](https://substack.com/redirect/b06603d2-805f-4884-bf6b-6650cf50d274?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and smarter than Leonardo da Vinci.
	+ Musk could ‘Drink piss [better than](https://substack.com/redirect/332e8ce4-be66-41d1-9f27-675cbfd78739?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) any human in history,’ Grok says.
* X plans to take over the [World Cup](https://substack.com/redirect/c8cf1c1a-16a5-4195-892a-ad1ab3f69d22?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) starting with Draw Day.
* French authorities charge X over [Holocaust-denial comments](https://substack.com/redirect/20be5f5a-4bcc-4927-b4c8-5480cbcdecbc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) by AI chatbot Grok.
* X Premium+ and Premium Business members now have [full access](https://substack.com/redirect/a0550c8a-f17f-446e-a04d-df1eb6138c3a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to the Handle Marketplace.
* Elon Musk [plans](https://substack.com/redirect/09cc0ec0-6255-44fc-9401-7487d2c4a76f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to [rebrand Grokipedia](https://substack.com/redirect/4c468196-1319-4373-95af-e076b474c5d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Encyclopedia Galactica.

> 🥀 Report Break: [Optimism among marketers](https://substack.com/redirect/44ab0b11-5f61-4b55-bf68-9f3c208b0acc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is falling due to economic volatility, with only 54% of industry professionals believing 2026 will be better than this year, according to WARC’s annual Marketer’s Toolkit report. That figure marks an 11 percentage-point drop from 2024.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!85wj!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F20a7b272-bbeb-4fe3-a619-d76e41822174_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/eadb7d44-dc2d-4049-a845-f2c91c52de54?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [Snapchat](https://substack.com/redirect/e9e793a9-0b3b-408d-80df-0868ae2fc40e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) launches ‘[Topic Chats](https://substack.com/redirect/44b5e355-3c9e-4706-a057-65fa920b1bf5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ for public conversations.
	+ Snapchat’s new research on how brands can better represent [multicultural audiences](https://substack.com/redirect/418ca6ac-ae15-4b6e-a73d-3944318d6fbb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
		- When a brand collaborates with a creator they follow, multicultural consumers will pay more attention to ads (54%) and see the brand as more trustworthy (52%) and authentic (49%).
	+ Snapchat launches ‘[Here For You](https://substack.com/redirect/230f17f8-c09f-4b34-84e1-2245dbaaf818?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ in Canada, expanding access to youth mental health resources.
	+ Snapchat [now available](https://substack.com/redirect/d7258e5e-b90d-494f-82b5-bf3931423968?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on Amazon Fire Tablets.
* [Bluesky](https://substack.com/redirect/a584619a-218f-4f21-afdc-9cd583375483?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) revamps its moderation and reporting tools with transparency in mind.
* Vox to partner with [Patreon](https://substack.com/redirect/58d4cc30-0884-40a7-bab2-0c23248b2596?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for new paywalled shows.
* Marketers move to bring [transparency](https://substack.com/redirect/614fab69-3aba-402a-9a52-957db4486eb9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to creator and influencer fees.
* [Epsilon audiences](https://substack.com/redirect/03f10acb-59b7-4d33-8260-7b7d6ee9b455?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) now available on Pinterest with improved match rates.
* Spotify will let you [import playlists](https://substack.com/redirect/77287321-194a-4a49-b243-198becd5e9aa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from other music platforms directly in its app.

* Wall Street Journal and Washington Post tech chiefs on [personalisation](https://substack.com/redirect/8961f6f9-d387-4731-afce-d25e36001ce8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for publishers.

> 🫸 Report Break: The ‘[2026 Media Budgets Survey](https://substack.com/redirect/10081dca-fced-4edd-b052-44c17f81d708?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ from ISBA, Ebiquity and the WFA [suggests](https://substack.com/redirect/c49f001e-9830-4606-b93a-831e846cf3be?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that 65 percent of UK marketers expect to see increased budgets next year, compared with the global average of 50 percent.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!0cUN!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F79af7349-0432-4bf9-83ad-f76d0fd2a362_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/8097a66b-8861-4bf9-95d6-bdc03b5bc99c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Microsoft makes *Zork I*, *II*, and *III* open source under [MIT License](https://substack.com/redirect/605f4b68-5986-4192-a334-d286cdf040dd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Talking to [Windows’ Copilot AI](https://substack.com/redirect/687f43d8-73ba-4aa8-b44f-50f48ac15ee2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) makes a computer feel incompetent.
* ‘[Clicks don’t pay the bills, pipeline quality does](https://substack.com/redirect/64ce6751-5235-4d49-9be0-5dfa933d814c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g),’ becomes LinkedIn’s case for its pricey ad prices.
* Critics scoff after Microsoft warns AI feature can [infect machines](https://substack.com/redirect/e41511cf-ba42-4c30-b7fc-44c79e63bf66?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and pilfer data.
* MI5 warns UK lawmakers [Chinese spies](https://substack.com/redirect/8118038d-5036-4106-b6ad-7684f90b79f4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) trying to reach them via LinkedIn.
* LinkedIn is giving users the opportunity to share a [two-month free trial](https://substack.com/redirect/eca0a7d7-15be-4bcd-9a53-f00150eb68b5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of a Premium subscription to new connections.
* Microsoft finally admits almost all major [Windows 11 core features](https://substack.com/redirect/cb40a596-032a-4985-b37c-46bf11d76f13?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are broken.
* Leaked documents shed light into how much [OpenAI](https://substack.com/redirect/0d65b16a-4269-4bfb-a565-0874e5f4fcc0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) pays Microsoft.
* Microsoft to retire its [Advertising mobile app](https://substack.com/redirect/7a488907-b07f-4454-a710-9f617d5c3061?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in early 2026.

> [Deloitte TMT Predictions](https://substack.com/redirect/b3543215-8db9-4abb-916a-0e5bb618b4b8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): In 2026, Deloitte predicts that the revenue growth of in-app micro-series will more than double, reaching $7.8 billion. Deloitte also predicts that the United States will account for half of global revenue in 2025, but its share will decline to 40% as other markets convert more views and downloads into cash.

* Verizon customers apparently starting to [pay the price of downsizing](https://substack.com/redirect/b6526a4d-3957-4b7c-b3d1-789709faccd2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) already.
* Zappi [introduces](https://substack.com/redirect/ed6cf204-ad28-469a-8cb4-4f2ec2014b22?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) its first brand health tracker.
* madSense launches [Agency OS](https://substack.com/redirect/718a4e3e-ea80-4c24-8f2d-437445ab84d8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), The first end-to-end AI ecosystem to see through adtech’s new walled gardens.

> 🧶 Report Break: Only 22% of marketers strongly feel they have enough data to justify value to their CFOs, according to [new research](https://substack.com/redirect/cfdb2871-3a48-4ac8-b4d1-081309f91cf0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from Perion and Advertiser Perceptions. 

> 62% say they need better tools to clearly demonstrate marketing’s impact on the bottom line. And nearly three in four selected a “unified tracking system” as a “must-have” for the future of marketing performance.

<h3 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: normal;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 4px 0 0;color: #777777;line-height: 24px;font-size: 18px;margin-top: 12px;">What was that? <div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: Spectral,sans-serif;font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><span>This newsletter is supported by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/97584f36-558c-4991-9929-ee1df63ebe81?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the affordable, always-on brand tracking dashboard. </span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/bec55781","text":"The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/981b918d-d627-4775-b6e3-932f319d5d90?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4f542216-2555-41e7-b662-6668a323e59e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/e2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":50683,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":true,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!VaNH!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8aed2f32-7e57-42b4-ba95-5d7b0124317e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok</a><span> to give users power to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/45063b12-ea4c-413b-bc62-a7158af553c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reduce amount of AI content</a><span> on their feeds.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2c769a5f-00c2-42f1-b362-49a25eb69d10?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bringing back DMs</a><span> after 6 years, but there’s a catch.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span> </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/63fbc66f-3a98-4e7a-b7a3-d9e1463e55c1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">X’s staying power in the U.S.</a><span>, despite competition.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>According to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1bf34328-6315-4c4b-8f2d-108b293d4027?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Pew’s latest social media report</a><span>, 21% of U.S. adults said they have used X, compared with only 8% who have used Threads, and 4% who have used Bluesky. </span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Adobe to buy digital marketing platform </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/61b62570-d00f-42bf-b89d-cf61faa7c3ac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Semrush</a><span> in $1.9 billion deal.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/49829708-4131-4ed1-9b9f-bd82482dc669?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">defeats</a><span> FTC’s antitrust case </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b1dda70a-03c0-41ca-a167-6f2f90478d7c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">alleging</a><span> social-media monopoly.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ca53d33c-5505-4770-9fcf-cbf547a1bee6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gemini 3</a><span> is here—and Google says it will make </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/43d05af6-04e6-4460-b2c8-1265a3358d3c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">search</a><span> smarter.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e1c59fa6-57ce-4435-a92a-6e5d661847ec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":48232,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!zM4F!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Pinterest is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/11c7e980-f2c9-43f9-8f03-8ae2baec45df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">leaning hard</a><span> into AI. The strategy is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6ac847d6-38f8-44a2-885d-ad6af830a3ba?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">alienating</a><span> its most dedicated users.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/59944bd6-b463-453a-8d7f-0056e9d2817e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">denies</a><span> Gmail reads your emails and attachments to train AI, but here’s how to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3678ce63-594a-4ab5-b4d7-420745ecb6d9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">opt-out </a><span>anyway.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Who is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/30a24c1a-4161-481c-a13f-83ea9785bb6b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OpenAI’s auditor</a><span>? (Update: it’s Deloitte).</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Owner of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1b7b6459-6649-494d-b02c-faf3e522a5c3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">U.K.’s Daily Mail</a><span> nears deal to buy rival Daily Telegraph.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Roblox will require </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4f8056d9-07da-4328-962b-80bf856ada7b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">age estimation to chat</a><span> starting next year.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Millions of Gen Zers are watching </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/48b12de2-5171-4f02-8efb-0cbcd072c24e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Hitler and Holocaust denial clips</a><span> on Instagram—right next to JPMorgan and U.S. Army ads.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meet the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/521ca11f-bc80-49bb-8e60-d1affb777b7e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">neo-Nazi targeting kids online</a><span>, teaching them to hate and to prepare to kill. </span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Thousands of french brands sue </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/66bf371c-795c-451b-b2f9-9c15119195ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Shein</a><span>, accusing it of unfair competition.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Paramount wins </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/872d0a0b-9cec-46ef-b2cc-355c0fb05ff5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Champions League rights</a><span> in UK &amp; Germany.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><em><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8493a1be-e720-4fab-93bf-f0a0986954c9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span> </span></em></p></blockquote>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;"><strong>Gen Z darling, Depop is betting on a broader audience</strong></h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Secondhand clothing resale platform, Depop recently launched their biggest ever brand campaign, “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5d4968ab-58ed-45c4-8edc-a2f2b86407ee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Where Taste Recognizes Taste</a><span>”, made with Uncommon Creative Studio. It’s the brand’s attempt to expand its customer base, trying to shift its positioning slightly away from purely “for Gen Zs” who have become the unofficial ambassadors of the platform.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d1f92aa0-163e-49a9-8e49-94ef49ea7063?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span> is tracking </span><strong>Depop</strong><span> in a few categories, and in the Women’s Secondhand Apparel (US) category, all of Depop’s funnel metrics have increased from November 2024 to October 2025. However, they are well and truly a challenger, with the lowest awareness in the category at 21% – compared to the likes of ebay at 80% and Poshmark at 70%.</span></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="700"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/164c15fd-3e75-4d32-8426-6b143ea993b8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/25f12daa-7edf-4b7c-ad8b-d34037d3d151\_1738x972.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":972,"width":1738,"resizeWidth":700,"bytes":547452,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/179710253?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F588cd3cf-d10e-4401-afba-1073e9722dee\_1738x992.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="307.59493670886076" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!y25Q!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F25f12daa-7edf-4b7c-ad8b-d34037d3d151\_1738x972.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Despite being lower on the overall awareness front, Depop have obviously found their people; these brand health metrics dramatically shift when you segment by the Gen Z demographic. Amongst 18 to 24-year-olds, Awareness increases to 57%, Consideration to 47% and Preference to 29%. We can also see that </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d1f92aa0-163e-49a9-8e49-94ef49ea7063?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">these metrics</a><span> have increased a lot in the last 12 months in this age group.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>In a marketplace category that can’t differentiate all too much on functionality and product, the value of brand is further elevated. Brand tracking tools like </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d1f92aa0-163e-49a9-8e49-94ef49ea7063?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span> are here to help marketers prove that big brand campaigns have paid off, and with the right audiences. Because no one can call brand fluffy, when you’ve got the growth receipts!</span></p>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://www.gotracksuit.com/au/lp/bigbets?utm\_campaign=Sponsored%20Partnerships&amp;utm\_source=email&amp;utm\_medium=sponsored\_placement&amp;utm\_term=the\_marketing\_meetup&amp;utm\_content=The%20Marketing%20Meetup","text":"See Tracksuit in action!","action":null,"class":null}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d1f92aa0-163e-49a9-8e49-94ef49ea7063?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;cursor: pointer;border: none;border-radius: 8px;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;text-align: center;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">See Tracksuit in action!</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Paramount, Comcast, Netflix </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1f7b39f7-756f-4d78-9f8e-c2a4913a8cef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">submit</a><span> </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1f7b39f7-756f-4d78-9f8e-c2a4913a8cef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bids</a><span> for Warner Bros. Discovery.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The spike in data centers is one of the main contributors to electricity demand and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1675423e-61b3-4f88-bf8c-d72787826259?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">blackout risks</a><span> this winter.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1b2c05e7-dcad-4d38-953e-2aaee0ac505b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">IAB</a><span>: Creator ad spending expected to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2a9d5a92-4fdd-407f-9ac2-3b89acda6053?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reach $37 Billion</a><span> this year.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>A Theory of Dumb: Why are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aa87ca19-a116-44d6-8fb3-107ed41adbda?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">IQ Scores</a><span> suddenly falling?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1c40c2a0-8193-4468-b196-8ed7fc9deadc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">surprised</a><span> investors with a ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/32e03410-5baa-4f7f-8a5a-d358ce13cca1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">half a trillion</a><span>’ forecast. It’ll come up at earnings.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Microsoft AI CEO puzzled by people being “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3987bf95-aabf-46be-9b78-45eeb0f30ecb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">unimpressed</a><span>” by AI.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google CEO: If an </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/98aaa25c-db2d-46ed-a547-d417754c9f88?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI bubble</a><span> pops, no one is getting out clean.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Delayed September report shows </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2931a044-f5fc-4206-b132-d832665b651e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">U.S. added 119,000 jobs</a><span>, more than expected; unemployment rate at 4.4%.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6b488f18-1926-4df9-b09e-8561b3326ae6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Study</a><span>: Anthropic AI Model ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ac4cb68b-b6ce-4682-95c4-30a91f1a6c33?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">turned evil</a><span>’ after hacking its training.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Age-verification laws </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7ee5ebb4-3c72-467c-bbef-5f140aa9f863?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">don’t keep minors away</a><span> from adult sites, study suggests.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
